      (function () {
        if (window.__navProgInit) return;
        window.__navProgInit = true;
        var timer, w = 0;
        function bar() { return document.getElementById('nav-progress'); }
        function start() {
          var b = bar(); if (!b) return;
          clearTimeout(timer); w = 8;
          b.classList.add('active'); b.style.width = '8%'; void b.offsetWidth;
          (function step() {
            w += (92 - w) * 0.14;
            var el = bar(); if (el) el.style.width = w + '%';
            timer = setTimeout(step, 180);
          })();
        }
        function done() {
          var b = bar(); if (!b) return;
          clearTimeout(timer); b.style.width = '100%';
          setTimeout(function () { var el = bar(); if (el) { el.classList.remove('active'); el.style.width = '0'; } }, 250);
        }
        // Transiciones de vista (ClientRouter): empieza al preparar la nav, termina al cargar.
        document.addEventListener('astro:before-preparation', start);
        document.addEventListener('astro:page-load', done);
        // Fallback para navegaciones completas (form posts, enlaces sin transiciÃ³n).
        document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
          var a = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('a') : null;
          if (!a) return;
          var href = a.getAttribute('href');
          if (!href || href.charAt(0) === '#') return;
          if (a.target === '_blank' || a.hasAttribute('download')) return;
          if (e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.shiftKey || e.altKey || e.button !== 0) return;
          try {
            var u = new URL(a.href, location.href);
            if (u.origin !== location.origin) return;
            if (u.pathname === location.pathname && u.search === location.search) return;
          } catch (_) { return; }
          start();
        }, true);
        window.addEventListener('beforeunload', start);
        window.addEventListener('pageshow', done);

        // Anuncios AdSense: tras cada swap de vista el body cambia sin recargar,
        // asÃ­ que pedimos anuncio para los slots nuevos aÃºn sin llenar.
        document.addEventListener('astro:page-load', function () {
          try {
            if (!window.adsbygoogle) return;
            var slots = document.querySelectorAll('ins.adsbygoogle:not([data-adsbygoogle-status])');
            for (var i = 0; i < slots.length; i++) (window.adsbygoogle = window.adsbygoogle || []).push({});
          } catch (_) {}
        });
      })();
